Also for the report: the SVO "set" severity. Let me think again about whether there is a real correctness issue beyond is_empty: The mask semantics: "Bitmask where bit i represents if child i is non-empty (1) or empty air (0)". After clearing, bit stays 1 with an air-leaf child. Consumers:
- is_empty: checks child_mask == 0 → false positive until collapse. But as analyzed, collapse only happens when ALL 8 children are identical leaves. Consider: root internal with 2 solid subtrees A and B in different octants, plus stale bits. Clear all voxels in A. A collapses to air leaf only if A's subtree is fully air after the clear — yes (collapse along path). Root's children: air leaf, internal B, 6 air leaves → not uniform → root stays Internal with mask bits: A's bit still 1 (stale!), B's bit 1, and any stale bits from earlier clears. So the root mask has 2 bits set but only one non-empty subtree. is_empty() → false — correct here (B is solid). But get() on A's octant: descends to the air leaf — returns (0,0,0,0) — same as empty. So the only harm: memory (tree bigger than needed) + is_empty false positives when ALL subtrees are cleared but stale bits remain in a node whose children are NOT all leaves... wait, if all subtrees are cleared, then every path collapses to leaf; root's children would be all air leaves → collapse → root Leaf air. Unless a subtree's collapse was prevented by stale bits making children non-uniform? Collapse checks VALUES not masks, so no. Hmm, so actually can is_empty() EVER be wrong? is_empty checks root: if root is Internal, child_mask == 0? For the root to be Internal with all-air leaves children — impossible because collapse would have converted it. For the root to be Internal, at least one child is an Internal (non-uniform) or... the collapse requires all 8 children be uniform leaves with equal values; if a child is an air leaf and others are air leaves → collapse. If one child is an internal → no collapse, and that internal is non-empty... wait, could an internal child be fully air? Only if its 8 children are all air leaves → then it would have collapsed. So an Internal node always has at least one non-uniform child... but non-uniform could mean "2 different solid leaves"? No wait — collapse requires all 8 children equal. If children are [air, air, ..., wall] — not uniform — Internal. If that single wall is cleared → children all air → collapse. So an Internal node that remains has at least one solid leaf somewhere. And mask bits: every octant that was ever written solid keeps a bit; a subtree can only be all-air if it collapsed... So actually stale bits only point to air LEAVES (directly, or internal subtrees? An octant child that is an internal whose subtree is all air would have collapsed to air leaf — so no). So is_empty() is never wrong! Let me re-verify: could root be Internal with all 8 children = air leaves? collapse_if_uniform runs after every set on the path — yes it would collapse. So the mask-stale issue: bits point at air leaves. Effect: get() descends one extra level (negligible), serializers emit a child pointer to an air leaf (harmless, minor waste: the air leaf still occupies 4 texels), SVDAG canonicalize recurses into all 8 children regardless of mask (no mask-based pruning — the mask isn't used to prune; it recurses into all 8 — meaning the stale bit costs nothing extra there... wait, canonicalize builds block of all 8 regardless; masks are only carried as data). Compression: two structurally identical subtrees with different stale mask bits fail to merge → compression loss.

Hmm wait — actually there IS a subtle is_empty bug: is_empty checks `SvoNode::Internal { child_mask, .. } => child_mask == 0`. A node that has stale bits pointing to air leaves — for the node to be Internal, at least one child is non-uniform... as established, an internal node always contains ≥1 solid leaf in its subtree. And the mask — could all mask bits be 0 while the node is Internal with a solid child? Mask bit set happens on the path when v_type != 0 — writing solid sets bits on every ancestor path. Clearing doesn't clear bits. So any solid leaf has all its ancestors' bits set. So mask bits are a superset of actual occupancy → mask==0 ⇒ no solid anywhere ⇒ ... if no solid anywhere, all children air leaves → collapsed. So mask==0 can't happen for an Internal (contradiction would mean collapse missed). Fine — is_empty is effectively correct.

BUT there's a worse variant of the mask bug: `child_mask |= 1 << octant_idx` when v_type != 0 — when WRITING SOLID into an octant whose child currently is an air leaf (from a previous clear) — the bit is already set; fine. And when writing solid into a *previously cleared* octant whose subtree had collapsed: the child is an air LEAF at this node level? If the octant's subtree collapsed to air leaf, then... the ancestor node: its children include this air leaf; the ancestor would only be Internal if some other child is non-uniform. The air-leaf child + writing solid → descend into the air leaf → split → fine.

OK so the mask-stale issue is Medium-Low: memory waste + serialization waste + SVDAG merge loss + violated documented invariant + get() descends into air leaves. Also **the collapse works only along the set() path** — wait, one more consideration: `collapse_if_uniform(node_idx)` is called at line 225 for the node we descended into, after recursion. So all ancestors get checked. OK.

Actually wait, there IS one more real issue with collapse: collapse checks only that all 8 children are leaves with equal values — if children are all air leaves, parent → Leaf air. But the PARENT's stale mask bit in the GRANDPARENT remains set (grandparent mask not updated — grandparent's collapse check examines children values: the collapsed parent is now a Leaf air; the grandparent may still be non-uniform). Fine.

So final severity for the mask issue: Medium (memory/compression/serialization waste + invariant violation; no crash; get()/is_empty() remain functionally correct).

Hmm, but actually — let me reconsider the serialization impact more carefully. octree_gpu_serializer emits internal node with child_mask — GPU traversal in the renderer (wasm_frontend) presumably uses child_mask to skip children. FINDINGS (sorted by severity):

A. Sparse Voxel Octree
1. [Medium] stale child_mask on air writes — sparse_voxel_octree.rs:202-204 (+is_empty 38-45). 
2. [Medium] arena never reclaims collapsed nodes — :170-181, :230-266. Unbounded growth under edit churn.
3. [Low] `1 << self.depth` shift-wraps for depth ≥ 32 — :54, :120; no validation.
4. [Low] get() conflates empty octant with air leaf (`Some((0,0,[0;3],0))`) — :91-93; callers can't distinguish "no data" (fine for air semantics, but breaks "empty vs data" queries and SVDAG canonicalization can't prune by mask).
5. [Low] per-voxel set() is O(depth) w/ up to 8·depth node allocations on first write; bulk building through set() is O(V·depth) vs O(V) bottom-up (build_octree/build_octree_direct already do it right; keep set() for sparse edits).

B. Path tracer
6. [Medium] O(V_solid·R·S) bake, no callers currently (dead/legacy) — but if revived, per-chunk ~200M steps; redundant hemisphere integrals per adjacent solid (up to 6×); transcendental per ray. 
7. [Low] origin placement contradicts comment (origin is 0.05 INSIDE solid; comment says adjacent air voxel) — asymmetric sampling; rays that exit through a neighboring solid waste work.
8. [Low] max_steps=30 cap → rays longer than 30 voxels return None → distance-limited lighting (rooms up to 16+ voxels wide; diagonals ~23+; corridor views >30) — underestimates distant lights.
9. [Low] fixed seed 42 per call → every chunk gets the identical direction sequence → spatially correlated noise/banding; seed should mix chunk coords.

C. Probe grid
10. [Low] sample_normalized doc claims trilinear; implementation is nearest-neighbor (.round()).
11. [Low] from_voxel_grid doc claims 1-probe halo duplication at boundaries; none implemented → potential seams between adjacent chunk probe volumes.
12. [Low] overlapping probe regions when target dims > source dims (`.max(z_start+1)`) — duplicated sampling; harmless.

D. CSG
13. [Medium] BSP build uses first polygon's plane without pivot selection (bsp.rs:32) → worst-case O(P²) build and O(P) recursion depth; clip_polygons recursion O(D) stack.
14. [Low] contains_point epsilon branch recurses into both children (bsp.rs:148-153) → O(2^k) for points on k coincident planes.
15. [Low] Solid::contains_point rebuilds the BSP per query (solid.rs:84-87) — documented, but invites O(V·P log P) in voxelizers; cache it.
16. [Low] clip_to re-clips each node's polygons against the full other tree — O((N_a+P_a)·D_b); fine at current scale.

E. Reality snapshot
17. [Medium] with_advanced_gate: clone stamps + drifts + consumed, BTreeMap rebuild O(S log S), re-sort of already-sorted consumed O(C log C) per crossing (reality.rs:369-412).
18. [Low] fingerprint() materializes a full Vec<u32> per call (reality.rs:423-426) — stream hash.
19. [Low] monotonic growth of stamps/drifts/consumed without eviction → transport/fingerprint cost grows with playtime (reality.rs:236-286).

F. Architecture
20. [Low] validate_architecture: O(H²)+O(O²)+O(O·H) via linear host() scans + O(L·O) (architecture.rs:635-716); fine for small assemblies; index hosts by id if large.
21. [Low] zone_at O(Z) per query; height_at per column → O(Z) per column (architecture.rs:471-487); fine at Z≤few.

G. Maze
22. [Low] per-iteration Vec::new for ≤4 neighbors (generate_maze.rs:55-65) — heap churn N times; use array.
23. [Low] loop-breaking opens outward walls on the grid border (generate_maze.rs:106-124) — knocks a hole in the outer shell (neighbor out of bounds).

H. Level builder
24. [Low] corner columns written twice by the wall h-loop (level_builder.rs:58-62); between-cell walls are 2 voxels thick by construction.

I. Voxel grid
25. [Low] 6 bytes/voxel across 4 parallel arrays with bounds-checked accessors; scalar light duplicates max(rgb) (voxel_grid.rs:199-283) — could drop light_data (compute on read) or store compact; also consider flat slice writes in hot loops.

Concurrency section for the domain:
- Inventory table: shared state = none. Statics: none (constants only). unsafe: none. thread_local: none. Atomics: none. RNG: local StdRng (path_tracer.rs:108) / caller-provided &mut R (generate_maze.rs:23). Cross-thread data: RealitySnapshot — immutable value type with canonical sorted vectors; binary-search queries are read-only; Clone used for evolution. 
- No races by construction; domain is Send+Sync (plain data; no interior mutability; SvoNode Copy etc.).
- Determinism: all generation pure functions of inputs + seeded RNG; mix64 deterministic; pitfalls: (a) path tracer seed fixed → same noise per chunk; (b) maze generator consumes caller RNG — if the same RNG is shared across chunks in sequence, chunk output depends on generation ORDER (deterministic per session but not per chunk-coordinate); recommend seeding per (seed, chunk coords) — this is a determinism-vs-parallelism note; (c) worker-snapshot identity: RealitySnapshot words transport has fingerprint — good for detecting races/corruption but note fingerprint() is not cryptographic.
- Note for parent: the concurrency surface lives outside domain (main.rs thread, worker code); domain contributes only immutable snapshots.

Ranked optimizations:
1. SVO: clear mask bits + reclaim arena (freelist) — bounds memory for editable octrees, fixes invariant (sparse_voxel_octree.rs).
2. Path tracer (if revived): per-air-voxel irradiance + scatter, or direct DDA from air voxel to light list; removes ~6-30× redundancy and transcedentals (path_tracer.rs).
3. RealitySnapshot: incremental updates (avoid full clones + re-sort + BTreeMap rebuild per crossing); stream fingerprint without Vec (reality.rs).
4. BSP: pivot-plane selection to bound depth O(log P); cache BspNode for repeated contains_point (bsp.rs, solid.rs).
5. Maze: stack-allocated neighbor buffer (generate_maze.rs) — micro but free.
6. Level builder / probe grid: hoist index math, share walls between cells, fix halo + trilinear docs (level_builder.rs, probe_grid.rs).

Big-O table — as computed above.

Counting from the file print earlier: line 1 is the doc comment `/// Represents a node...`? The first printout shows lines without numbers. Let me just number the first 110 lines to get precise line numbers.
    1| /// Represents a node inside the Sparse Voxel Octree (SVO).
    2| /// Implements a linearized, GPU-friendly design stored in a flat arena array.
    3| #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
    4| pub enum SvoNode {
    5|     /// Leaf node storing actual voxel attributes
    6|     Leaf {
    7|         voxel_type: u8,
    8|         color: u32,
    9|         light_rgb: [u8; 3],
   10|         face_occlusion: u8,
   11|     },
   12|     /// Internal parent node directing traversal to 8 children
   13|     Internal {
   14|         /// Base index in the arena vector where the 8 children nodes are stored contiguously.
   15|         child_base_index: u32,
   16|         /// Bitmask where bit `i` represents if child `i` is non-empty (1) or empty air (0).
   17|         child_mask: u8,
   18|     },
   19| }
   20| 
   21| /// The Sparse Voxel Octree aggregate root entity.
   22| #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
   23| pub struct SparseVoxelOctree {
   24|     pub root: usize,
   25|     pub nodes: Vec<SvoNode>,
   26|     pub depth: u32,
   27|     pub world_size: f32,
   28| }
   29| 
   30| impl SparseVoxelOctree {
   31|     /// Creates a new empty Sparse Voxel Octree.
   32|     pub fn new(depth: u32, world_size: f32) -> Self {
   33|         let root_node = SvoNode::Leaf {
   34|             voxel_type: 0,
   35|             color: 0,
   36|             light_rgb: [0; 3],
   37|             face_occlusion: 0,
   38|         };
   39|         Self {
   40|             root: 0,
   41|             nodes: vec![root_node],
   42|             depth,
   43|             world_size,
   44|         }
   45|     }
   46| 
   47|     /// Checks if the SVO is entirely empty (air).
   48|     pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
   49|         match self.nodes[self.root] {
   50|             SvoNode::Leaf { voxel_type, .. } => voxel_type == 0,
   51|             SvoNode::Internal { child_mask, .. } => child_mask == 0,
   52|         }
   53|     }
   54| 
   55|     /// Queries the SVO for voxel attributes at integer coordinates `(x, y, z)`.
   56|     pub fn get(&self, x: u32, y: u32, z: u32) -> Option<(u8, u32, [u8; 3], u8)> {
   57|         let max_coord = 1 << self.depth;
   58|         if x >= max_coord || y >= max_coord || z >= max_coord {
   59|             return None;
   60|         }
   61|         self.get_recursive(self.root, x, y, z, self.depth)
   62|     }
   63| 
   64|     fn get_recursive(
   65|         &self,
   66|         node_idx: usize,
   67|         x: u32,
   68|         y: u32,
   69|         z: u32,
   70|         depth: u32,
   71|     ) -> Option<(u8, u32, [u8; 3], u8)> {
   72|         if node_idx >= self.nodes.len() {
   73|             return None;
   74|         }
   75| 
   76|         match self.nodes[node_idx] {
   77|             SvoNode::Leaf {
   78|                 voxel_type,
   79|                 color,
   80|                 light_rgb,
   81|                 face_occlusion,
   82|             } => Some((voxel_type, color, light_rgb, face_occlusion)),
   83|             SvoNode::Internal {
   84|                 child_base_index,
   85|                 child_mask,
   86|             } => {
   87|                 if depth == 0 {
   88|                     return None;
   89|                 }
   90| 
   91|                 let bit = depth - 1;
   92|                 let octant_x = (x >> bit) & 1;
   93|                 let octant_y = (y >> bit) & 1;
   94|                 let octant_z = (z >> bit) & 1;
   95|                 let octant_idx = (octant_z << 2) | (octant_y << 1) | octant_x;
   96| 
   97|                 // Check if the octant is active in the child mask
   98|                 if (child_mask & (1 << octant_idx)) == 0 {
   99|                     return Some((0, 0, [0; 3], 0)); // Empty air
  100|                 }
  101| 
  102|                 let child_node_idx = child_base_index as usize + octant_idx as usize;
  103|                 let mask = (1 << bit) - 1;
  104|                 self.get_recursive(child_node_idx, x & mask, y & mask, z & mask, depth - 1)
  105|             }
  106|         }
  107|     }
  108| 
  109|     /// Sets voxel attributes at coordinate `(x, y, z)`. Splits nodes and collapses uniform subtrees recursively.
  110|     pub fn set(
====== voxel_grid index area
  196|         let size = width * height * depth;
  197|         Self {
  198|             width,
  199|             height,
  200|             depth,

  202|             light_data: vec![0; size],
  203|             light_rgb: vec![[0; 3]; size],
  204|             face_occlusion: vec![0; size],
  205|         }
  206|     }
  207| 
  208|     pub fn width(&self) -> usize {
  209|         self.width
  210|     }
  211|     pub fn height(&self) -> usize {

  213|     }

  218|     fn index(&self, x: usize, y: usize, z: usize) -> Option<usize> {
  219|         if x < self.width && y < self.height && z < self.depth {
  220|             Some(y * (self.width * self.depth) + z * self.width + x)
  221|         } else {
  222|             None
  223|         }
  224|     }

  228|             self.data[idx] = voxel_type;
  229|         }
  230|     }

  232|     pub fn get(&self, x: usize, y: usize, z: usize) -> u8 {

  234|             self.data[idx]
  235|         } else {

  237|         }

  240|     /// Sets a neutral (white) light level: all three channels get `level`.
  241|     pub fn set_light(&mut self, x: usize, y: usize, z: usize, level: u8) {
  242|         self.set_light_rgb(x, y, z, [level; 3]);
  243|     }
  244| 
  245|     pub fn get_light(&self, x: usize, y: usize, z: usize) -> u8 {
  246|         if let Some(idx) = self.index(x, y, z) {
  247|             self.light_data[idx]
  248|         } else {
  249|             0
  250|         }
  251|     }
  252| 
  253|     /// Sets colored light (0-15 per channel); the scalar level becomes the
  254|     /// max of the channels.
  255|     pub fn set_light_rgb(&mut self, x: usize, y: usize, z: usize, rgb: [u8; 3]) {
  256|         if let Some(idx) = self.index(x, y, z) {
  257|             self.light_rgb[idx] = rgb;
  258|             self.light_data[idx] = rgb[0].max(rgb[1]).max(rgb[2]);
  259|         }
  260|     }
  261| 
  262|     pub fn get_light_rgb(&self, x: usize, y: usize, z: usize) -> [u8; 3] {

  264|             self.light_rgb[idx]
  265|         } else {
  266|             [0; 3]
  267|         }
  268|     }

  270|     /// Stores the six-bit neighbor-occupancy mask for one voxel.
  271|     pub fn set_face_occlusion(&mut self, x: usize, y: usize, z: usize, mask: u8) {
  272|         if let Some(idx) = self.index(x, y, z) {
  273|             self.face_occlusion[idx] = mask & FACE_OCCLUSION_MASK;
  274|         }
